A test compares old and new over random pairs on the game's valley and the vivarium's zone. **S2** the A* arena a thread (`map::Arena`): a flat square of the sight, each slot stamped by the search that last reached it, nothing zeroed, the open set kept; the same expansion order, ties and path. **S3** the skirt's five windows likewise (`map::SkirtArena`), the sweeps reading the cells marked, sorted into the square's order. **S4** a straight walk's sidesteps answered by a flood of the 9 × 9 square (the bits of one word) before the small A*, whose "no" the flood gives exactly. **S5** the bodies grow once a day (`World::grow_due` raised by every change the growth reads: a body added, carried, set down, orphaned or dead, a pregnancy, the life rule, the content, a load) and the carriers' state is built only when some body is carried. **S6** the gatherer keeps its crop's number (`Work::crop`, never stored), asks rate and `ripe_of_crop` by it, shares `gives` (`Arc<str>`, written as text: the snapshot's JSON unchanged), records a pick in place and adds the grams to the load. **S7** the vivarium's continent kept on disk (`vivarium::continents`): a cache of the vivarium tool, nothing of the game's (the server keeps its continents in `continents.sqlite`, D28, untouched), the bytes of `Continent::to_bytes` under `${XDG_CACHE_HOME:-~/.cache}/soul-sim/continents/continent-<seed>-<side>-g<generator>.bin` (60 MB, `std::fs`, no dependency), read back by the next run of the same key, on by default for the vivarium's world alone, `--no-continent-cache` to bear it anew; the same pages either way (a test, and D28's amendment of 19 November). **Measured** (release, the machine loaded by other agents all day, load 4 to 8: reference and new alternated, the probe's two passes read at their minimum, shares read within one run): the pair's process CPU with trials **37.8 → 20.0 s** (two alternations: 37.8 / 47.3 against 20.6 / 20.0, about −47 %, the birth's 5.5–6.5 s gone to 0.3 s in it); the loop by the probe (the same timers on every build) `World::step` **12.06 → 5.63 s (−53 %)**, `Brains::think` 15.1 → 12.1 s (the same code: the noise of the day), the step's share of the loop 44 → 32 %, the loop −35 %; the step commit by commit, S1 12.06 → 7.92 s (−34 %: the pre-test), S2 → 6.76 (−15 %), S3 → 6.69, S4 → 6.88 (within the noise of ±0.2 s), S5 → 6.04 (−12 %), S6 → 5.78 (−4 %), S7 → 5.63; the birth **5.9 → 0.32 s** (S7: the continent read in 0.3 s instead of born in 5.5 to 13 s by the load), peak RSS 132 → 122 MB on the pair; 250 days 429 → 175 s of process CPU under load, the page the same. Protocol and the game's storage unchanged; no dependency; `plan/` touched by a test-only re-export, the ignored `plan::net` tests run by hand (one fails on main already, `SeaAsLand` on two marsh windows, the same two on the branch). Not done, by the lead's word: S12 (the page's picture) refused; S8 is the brain's; S13 (needs and walks by event) later; the behaviour rules S9–S11 are D54's.

### D52 — The laboratory: series of runs created, followed and read from a local page
- **Reason**: the project lead, 22 November 2026, as the simulation phase opens: "on va bosser pas mal sur les simulations… des GUI qui nous créent facilement des versions… pages HTML, le plus simple… des comptes rendus, des tableaux, des courbes, des visuels… des comparaisons." A series of vivarium runs (D41) was launched from a JSON plan written by hand and read from an HTML synthesis found in a cache folder; the lead wants to ask a question of the simulation, launch twenty seeds with and without a rule, follow them, and read the answer, without a terminal.
- **Consequences**:
  - **A local tool in the repository, `tools/lab/`**, a small Python server on the standard library alone (`http.server`, `json`, `subprocess`, `threading`), serving on `127.0.0.1` only, opening nothing by itself (the lead opens the URL it prints). Its pages are plain HTML, CSS and a little JavaScript, in French, with no external resource: nothing goes outward, no dependency enters the repository, the game never runs it and depends on nothing in it. Three screens: **Nouvelle série** (a form: the question, the world, the people, the days with the year of 50 days shown, the seeds, with and without trials, the cadence with the warning that 10 is the game's and ten times slower, the runs at a time; "Relancer" a plan and "Dupliquer en changeant…"), **En cours** (every series with its live table, refreshed every two seconds from a JSON endpoint, a stop button with the confirmation built into the page, the final line and the links to the synthesis and to each run's page), **Lire / Comparer** (reports, tables, curves and comparisons between series).
  - **`soul-runs` absorbed**: the lab keeps the local launcher's plan format (`question`, `binary`, `content`, `out`, `runs` of `name`, `args`, `seeds`, `with_and_without_trials`) and its output layout (`<runs-dir>/plans/<name>.json`; `<runs-dir>/<name>/serie.json`, one `NAME.log`, `NAME.html`, `NAME.exit` per run, `synthese.html` at the end), so the plans already written keep working in both. The lab adds a `lab` key to the plan (the form's fields, the jobs) that `soul-runs` ignores.
  - **The runs belong to the lab process**: it holds their PIDs and ends them only on the user's button or when it exits; a series launched elsewhere is shown from its files and never stopped from here. The vivarium binary is not built by the lab: if it is missing or being rebuilt, the series waits and retries.
  - **In two pull requests**: the first builds the two first screens (this decision); the third screen comes once the vivarium emits its report as JSON beside the HTML page (the next pull request on `server/vivarium`), so that reading and comparing parse data, never pages.
  - **Amended 22 November 2026, after the first pull request** (the lead: "c'est toi qui dois pouvoir utiliser cet outil. Moi, ce que je veux, c'est comparer… tu fais de la modification, et du coup on relance les runs"). The lab is the AI session's tool to launch and the lead's tool to read and compare. Every series records its **version** in `serie.json` (the git commit and branch of the binary's worktree, "inconnu" without one, and a sentence of what changed), shown everywhere the series appears; a command line `lab.py run PLAN --change "…"` launches a series detached from any server; three **standard plans** live in the repository under `tools/lab/plans/` and are run after every change of the simulation; **comparing means first the same plan under two versions**, run by run (same seed, same with/without), seeds and with/without being the second control. The discoveries are read as a story of techniques per tribe, from the kinds the data names, never from a list in the lab.
  - **Amended on 23 November 2026 — the run page laid out (lab 5; the brief `content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lab/BRIEF-lab-5-layout.md`, two tiny tries read by the project lead: "ça va mieux… on va rester là-dessus").** The run page reads in five groups, each opened by its summary: **Résumé, Les gens, Où ils vivent, Ce qu'ils ont appris, Chronique**, then **Annexes**. A summary is never a paragraph: a verdict line, then points of a bold figure and half a sentence (4 to 6 at the top, 2 to 4 per section and per table, at most 15 words each), with an arrow and a small curve when the figure is a series in time; the points are chosen and written from the data, never by hand. Each tribe keeps one colour, a letter and a side of the map everywhere (tiles split A | B, tables, the chronicle's edge, the map). The tables show 4 or 5 columns with units and 10 rows, "plus de colonnes" and "voir les N" on demand, sort on click, and have no empty cell. The chronicle is read a year at a time, filtered by kind, the same events of a day grouped. Every name opens a card of the person's life (a strip of the years, trials, lessons, what they told at the fire). The map draws, under a year cursor, the presence, the camps dated and the places known that year, seen or heard. The heavy pieces come from the lab on demand, so the page itself stays light: `/morceau/<serie>/<run>?quoi=carte|annexes`, `/fiche/<serie>/<run>?id=N`, `/chronique/<serie>/<run>?an=N` (JSON), all read from the run's data file. Measured on the 20-year fire run of seed 1: the page went from 4.72 MB to 0.24 MB, the map coming at 1.87 MB, the annexes at 1.73 MB, a year of the chronicle at 0.06 to 0.33 MB. The charts and the map follow the dark theme. Still the standard library alone, no dependency, no external resource.
  - **Amended on 23 November 2026 — one page (lab 6; the brief `content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lab/BRIEF-lab-6-one-page.md`, a first step read by the project lead: "c'est déjà mieux", with three corrections to the home's cards).** The vivarium's page is folded into the run page, whose summaries stay first: **the factors** as cards by theme read from `meta.factors` (D41); **the maps** as two canvases side by side, each choosing among six kinds (terrain and relief, food by season, water, noons, routes and camps, deaths and births), sharing a year slider, pan, zoom and hover, their data loaded on demand; **the curves** as small multiples with one shared hairline; **food and tribes year by year** as big figures, trend points, a strip of the years with its remarkable years marked, the table folded; **the chronicle** opened by its trends and ten notable facts chosen from the data, one of each kind in turn; **ages and families**, a pyramid at the start and at the end and the families as folded cards. The home and the series pages are **compact cards**, closed by default (title, day, launch → end, runs, the purpose's first sentence or "but non écrit"), opened on a click for the verdict, what changed, the version and the runs; sorting, filtering, search and grouping by day. A plan gains `title` (the form's **Titre**; the standard plans have one). A series' aggregates and curves are grouped by what differs between its runs (trials, fire, or both), no longer by trials alone. The new pieces are `/morceau/<serie>/<run>?quoi=fond|carte-base|carte-an&an=N|courbes|relief|nourriture|annees` and `/api/resultats?serie=`. The run page weighs some 330 KB (the years table, the maps and the curves come on demand). Colours always come with a word or a sign. Reason: the lead reads one page per run and one list of series; the vivarium's long page and the lab's run page said the same things twice.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 — the pages in plain words (the project lead on the series page: "Il n'y a rien qui va"; the spec he validated the same day).** **The series page** is the series' question (`plan.question`), its answer under it (`plan.answer`, a sentence or two written when the series ends; "Réponse : pas encore écrite" until then) and **one table**, a row a run: at the start, births, deaths (of hunger), alive at the end, lived in all, couples together at dawn (`nights_apart`), the median distance from the camp at noon in spring (`positions` against `years[].camp`), homes changed (`home_changes`); a header's sentence explains each column. A row is named by the run's `note` (its seed added when the note does not say it), else by the settings that differ between the series' runs, and the seed. A run may name its **reference** (`ref`, `"series/run"`, another series' run as well): the references come first, marked, and each run's cells are coloured against its own reference, green better and red worse, the direction fixed per column (more births, alive, lived and couples together, fewer deaths), a gap of 5 % or less (2 at least, 3 points for the couples) left uncoloured as noise; the distance and the homes changed are never coloured, neither more nor less being better in itself. The series-wide means, the version and two-runs comparisons and the times line are gone; the computing time of each run (`meta.timing.wall_s`) is in small at the bottom, hidden when unknown. **The run page** opens with a breadcrumb (Labo › the series › the run) and a back button, one summary line (at the start, births, deaths by cause, alive, lived in all, the simulated days and age-years), then always the same parts in this order, each opened by one plain sentence: the map (the six maps and their year slider of lab 6), the population, births and deaths on a frieze, the ages (pyramids in age-years), the couples, the homes changed, the chronicle; the rest (the factors, the other curves, the years, what they learnt, the tables) is folded at the bottom. The summaries by points of lab 5 leave the page. `ref` and `plan.answer` are optional: an older series opens as before, a run without the newer keys shows "—" (`docs/10-vivarium-data.md`, the series file). Reason: the lead reads one table to know what a series found, and must know at once where he is.

### D53 — The rules of thought that make the vivarium light
- **Reason**: the project lead, 22 November 2026, reading the brain audit of the day (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/allegement/audit-cerveau-2026-11-22.md`, §4 (b): the steps that change behaviour): "je suis pour les suggestions… rentrer le plus de choix possible, qui ne dérangent pas le jeu et qui n'ont pas de conflit ensemble… On va vraiment vouloir enchaîner les simulations au maximum… si on peut trouver des triches sur le chemin, on va pas hésiter." After lightening 2 (D51 amended) the brain was still 59 % of the vivarium's loop, and three habits made it: a body looked for plants to the horizon (18 chunks a look, each placed at some milliseconds), remembered 256 bushes re-read in the world at every choice, and thought of hunger 90 to 115 times a day. The vivarium's 20-year run must go towards 5 minutes. Unlike D51's steps these **change behaviour**; each passes the lead's test, **a perception or a gesture is fine, a disguised outcome rule is not**, and nothing is tuned to a result.
- **Consequences**:
  - **Four rules, one commit each**, the new numbers placeholders in `content/needs.ron`, section `thought` (D30), today's values read from there, the D50 ways (`worth_half_tiles`, `sleep_out`, `stock_kj`, `home`) untouched: these rules are the substrate they act on.
    - **S11 — plants seen at `plant_sight_tiles` (30), not at the horizon**; the sight for water and for others stays the terrain's (`movement.ron`, 60 on grass). One does not tell the berries of a bush at the horizon. On screen: bodies rake their land tighter and discover it more slowly, mostly in the first days.
    - **S10 — a hysteresis on hunger**: hungry from `HUNGRY` (50), a body counts as fed again only under `fed_under` (30). Fed to 49 it was hungry at 50 a quarter of an hour later, each time a thought, a mouthful and a walk. The judge's "hungry at nightfall" (D50 b) stays at 50. On screen: it finishes its meal instead of leaving at the first mouthful, and comes back to eat a few times a day.
    - **S8 — the plant to gather is chosen once in `plant_choice_hours` (1)**: on its way to one (`Mind::chose_at`) it sticks to it unless it is spent or out of reach, left alone, or thirst or hunger calls; a gather ended, a stop short, a drink or a meal make a fresh choice. One looks at the pantry once in a while. On screen: a body keeps its target a few minutes while a neighbour plant just overtook it by a gram.
    - **S9 — a memory of places, not bushes**: a body remembers the squares of `place_tiles` (16, a quarter of a chunk) where it saw trees and plants bearing food, with what kinds, how many at most, how many held a step's worth of food when it last looked and what an hour of the fullest gave (`brain::Place`, `brain::Seen`: one sees the fruit on a bush, not a bush), the `places_remembered` (48) worth most from where it last looked. The choice is a memory alone (the fullest plant seen there, less the farther by its way `worth_half_tiles`, more plants holding breaking ties); standing at the place it looks again, refreshes what it holds, picks the plant worth most by what is left on it (a few dozen plants read instead of 256) or goes to another place remembered fuller; a place found bare is spent for the crop's year, one still ripening or with a refused plant left alone an hour (`RETRY_HOURS`), one it could not walk to left for the day. The home trial of D50 b counts the plants known bearing by place. Nobody remembers every bush. On screen: it walks to a corner it saw full and hesitates a little there. A first S9 remembered only that a kind stood at a place: measured, it tripled the thoughts (a body toured its 48 places in spring and found crumbs at each, D48) and ran slower; what the plants held was added to the look the same day, as the perception it is.
  - **Measured** (release, `--world vivarium --people 25 --tribes 2 --days 250 --cadence 1 --seed 1`, with and without `--no-trials`, CPU time of the process on a loaded machine, so read the order of magnitude, not the digit; the reference is lightening 2's binary): with trials, CPU of the process 363 s (reference) → 199 s after S11 → 128 s after S10 → 126 s after S8 (pages identical to S10's) → 144 s after S9; without trials 406 → 136 → 149 → 190 → 122 s (the no-trials runs of S8 ran under a load of 10, read the order). Over 250 days with trials: alive 65 → 70 → 64 → 64 → 69 of 72–75 people, deaths 7 → 5 → 7 → 7 → 4 (of illness mostly, 1 of hunger before S9, none after), births 21 → 23 → 20 → 20 → 21, trials kept 167 → 114 → 148 → 148 → 118, gathered 1.77 → 1.75 → 1.81 → 1.81 → 1.81 GJ a person; without trials alive 71 → 74 → 77 → 77 → 76, gathered 1.72 → 1.62 → 1.60 → 1.60 → 1.61 GJ a person. S11 carries the time gain (the chunks placed), S10 the thoughts (456,000 a 250-day run instead of about 1.3 million); S9 is no gain on this pair of 25 a camp (the look now reads what each plant in sight holds) and changes the gathering gesture the lead asked for. The 20-year pair (`--days 1000`), reference and branch run side by side: wall **1,109 → 671 s with trials (−40 %)** and **1,022 → 669 s without (−35 %)**, CPU 1,098 → 664 s and 1,020 → 667 s, both under a load of 5–10; outcomes with trials alive 54 → 62 of 82 → 91 people, deaths 28 → 29 (illness 24 → 27, hunger 1 → 0, cold 2 → 0, thirst 0 → 1, childbirth 1 → 1), births 32 → 41, trials kept 822 → 656, gathered 6.4 → 6.2 GJ a person; without trials alive 56 → 83 of 90 → 122, deaths 34 → 39 (illness 29 → 36, hunger 3 → 0, cold 2 → 1, childbirth 0 → 2), births 40 → 71, gathered 5.8 → 5.0 GJ a person (more mouths); thoughts 1.72 → 2.06 million and 2.14 → 2.74 million over the twenty years, about the same a body-day with more bodies; peak memory 352 → 412 MB and 424 → 528 MB (more people, more land placed). The pages differ from main's by construction (behaviour). Five minutes is not reached: the 20-year run went from 18 to 11 minutes, the birth (13 s) and the world's step the larger part now. Outcomes over 250 days (alive, dead by cause, births, trials kept, gathered kJ a person) stay within the run's own noise; none of the rules halves the gathered food or doubles the deaths. S8 changed nothing on the pair (pages identical to S10's): within an hour the fresh choice was the kept one.
  - The pages change with each rule (D51's invariant does not apply: these are behaviour); protocol and storage unchanged; no dependency; `plan/` untouched.
  - **Amended on 23 November 2026 (lot 5b) — the night's notebook.** For D50's home trial (the project lead: "ils doivent se souvenir des endroits où vivre et choisir eux-mêmes"), each body keeps at most eight places of `place_tiles` where it slept (`brain::Lived`): at each dawn the place where it lies is noted with its day, lain down hungry, thirsty or cold or not, the tiles walked, what it gathered in per cent of its need. Every note fades by `trials.lived_keep_permille` (950: a night weighs half after two weeks), so a place that turned bad loses its grade; the least lived place gives its room to a new one. Grade: the fewest hard nights, then the least walked, then the most gathered. Nothing in it names water or plants: only the body's own day grades a place. **Amended on 24 November 2026**: the notebook also holds the places of the day, each with only what was done there: a place of `place_tiles` where it drank, with the thirst it quenched; a grove of `trials.grove_tiles` (64) where it gathered, with what it gathered, so gathering scattered in a grove adds up as drinking at one water does; the night's place with what the night left lacking and the day's walk; each with what walking there and back cost, which living there would save. Every sum is a `brain::Sense` (food, water, warmth today) in per mille of a day of that need.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 — seen, not reached** (the study `piege-b-2026-11-24.html` in `content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/`; the project lead's go of 24 November 2026, "On y va"). Tribe B died twice by 30 or more on a river's north bank, walking each day toward places it saw bearing on the far bank (a body sees plants 30 tiles off, the river is 30 to 45 wide and crosses the map): a place it could not walk to was left for the day only, so each dawn it was the best again, told and called again. Now a walk to a place, or to a plant of a place the body does not stand on, that ends **barred by water or rock** (`BodyBlocked`, `MoveRefused` with `Blockage::Water` or `Blockage::Rock`) marks the place **seen, not reached** (`Place::barred`: its own draft, the day, until told; then, like a place, an access to the `Record::Barred` it told or heard, held in the well, D55, and released when the mark clears or the body dies); a walk stopped with no way in sight (`Blockage::NoWay`: a trunk, the map's edge, an order refused, an obstacle beyond the look) keeps the old rule, the place left for the day. The mark lasts; a marked place is no longer chosen, called, answered nor told as food. It is told at the fire like a denial (`Record::Barred`, apart from the slots, `denied_per_night` at most), and each present who holds the place and has not stood on it since marks it too. It clears only when the body itself stands on the place (`Place::stood`, noted at each thought): knowing that a mate was there does not tell how to get there (the project lead, 24 November 2026: "Une personne qui trouve le chemin vient le dire. Une personne qui ne trouve pas fait demi-tour, revient au camp, et essaie d'autres choses. Comme dans la vraie vie."). A perception lived and passed on, nothing about rivers or fords decided by rule; the walk itself is unchanged (D32, D54: skirt once to the farthest point within five sixths of sight, look once more, take a way seen, otherwise stop). The project lead's rule of 24 November 2026 replaced a first version where any barred walk marked the place and the mark faded after a season: water and rock mark for good, anything else only for the day. A group whose place was barred now ends `blocked`, no longer told as bare.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 a) — company, a sense of the notebook.** The project lead's rules of 24 November 2026: company is a **need of the body felt only at night at the fire**, where anyone at one's fire counts and family weighs no more; it **saturates fast**, about two others enough for a loner and six for a sociable, more adding nothing; **a night fully alone weighs at most one day of need**, for the most sociable, and less as sociability falls; it **grades places only and never gives an order** (no trigger, no interruption, no change to the day's decisions); nothing makes crowds good or bad by rule, food is the counterweight. Built as the notebook was designed, one more `brain::Sense`, `Company`: at dawn, before the night is noted (`Brains::feel_company`), each grown body's night lacks `company.alone_permille × s / 100` per mille of a day (s its sociability, 0 to 100) times what is missing of enough (`enough_loner` 2 at s = 0 to `enough_sociable` 6 at s = 100, in a straight line), the others at its fire counted, none at no fire (slept out, or alone at its camp); `fare` reads it with the other losses. Only with the fire lit: without it there is no gathering to feel. The **sociability is innate**, drawn even from 0 to 100 from the run's seed and the body's id (salt `SOCIABILITY`), never inherited, fixed for life. Placeholders in `content/needs.ron`, section `company` (D30); absent, nobody feels it. Decided alone: the even draw, the run's seed rather than the map's (the will is drawn from the map's, the same in every run seed), an infant feeling nothing, the loss counted at dawn just before the night is noted.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 b) — a spouse weighs more in the move's company.** The night's company still counts anyone alike. Only when a body weighs a move called at the fire (D56) does its spouse count as `company.spouse_counts` companions (3, a D30 placeholder): here if it stays, there if it said it goes. `Company::lacking_hundredths` reads the others in hundredths of a body for this. Reason: the project lead, 24 November 2026, after first saying couples were not as we know them: keep the existing couples (D49), because modelling looser early-human bonds would be complicated and would touch sensitive subjects; a spouse's absence is felt more, so a spouse who goes pulls the other without forcing it.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 b) — the home graded by what lies within reach.** The project lead, 24 November 2026: what matters for where to live is being near water and plenty of fruits and plants; the cold is the same everywhere until fire and caves come. The grade (`brain::fare`) was the night's fare (hunger and thirst at nightfall, the cold, the company, the day's walk): a home with groves all around still graded near −2000, since a body lies down hungry whatever its home, and nothing in it said what lay near. Now a place `c` (the home or any candidate, by the same formula) grades, in per mille of a day: **food**, each noted grove's food a day (its gains over its nights and days) less the walk from `c` there and back (the body's cost of a thousand tiles, `Mind.walk_kilotile`, felt at nightfall), the positive ones only, the best first, summed and capped at a day (1000); plus **water**, the best noted drinking place's a day less its walk, capped at a day; less the **night's losses** of the senses felt only at night (the cold, the company), from the place's own nights if it has a night's worth, else from the home's. Hunger and thirst at nightfall are still noted, no longer counted. Which sense is got by day and how its places add (`Sense::reach`: food from all, water from the best) is a property of the sense, not a rule that water is good: a place grades only by what the body itself got near it, and a new sense plugs in the same way. Measured (seed 9, five years): the homes grade 575 to 1184, medians about 1000 to 1086 each year.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 b) — walking counted in time too.** The project lead, 24 November 2026: an hour walking is an hour not gathering. In the reach grade, each walk there and back from a place to a grove or a drinking place costs its energy, as before, plus **the share of the day's light it takes**, at the body's own pace on the ground it lay on at its last nightfall (`Mind.day_tiles`: the tiles it walks through the season's hours of light), **valued at what this body gathers in a day** (`Mind.gathered`: what it gathered its last days, in per mille of a day of need, faded as its notes, `trials.lived_keep_permille`); a body that gathered nothing lately values a day at `trials.idle_day_permille` (1000, a D30 placeholder). Reason: energy alone cost about 13 per mille of a day per thousand tiles, so a place's distance from its groves and water barely changed its grade (the first control run: 7 homes moved in five years); a perception of the body's own time does. Choices made by the AI session: the pace on the ground where it lies at nightfall, not along each path (the notebook does not keep the paths); every day counts in the average, a day gathering nothing too; the time counts only once the body has felt a nightfall.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 b) — no rounding.** `lived_best` ranked the places by their grade in tens, so a place could win while beating the home by less than the margin (a gain of 5 per mille was told 243 times in a control run). It now ranks by the exact gain less the move's cost (D56) and keeps the best only above the margin.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 — one yardstick, judged fairly; the push and the surprise.** The synthesis (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/synthese-penseurs-demenager-2026-11-24.md`, point 6), validated by the project lead: (1) **the cold leaves the grade** while nothing gives warmth (`Sense::graded`; the lead: "kif-kif"): it is the same everywhere and brought only the season into the grade; (2) **a notebook by season**: each place keeps a note for each season it was lived in, a note fading only on the days of its season (`Lived::season`), so a home is judged against the same season elsewhere ("là-bas, les hivers étaient ainsi"); a place with no note of a season is graded by all its seasons summed; the eight places are kept, the least lived this season giving its room first, never the home's place nor the old home while a move is judged (a note out of season does not fade, so an old place would otherwise keep its room for ever: the first control run's new homes were pushed out of their own notes every night); (3) **an empty visit counts**: a planned place found with nothing to gather is a day of nothing in the notes of its grove, at a place already noted only; (4) **food is no longer capped at a day** in the grade, so abundance shows (water stays at a day: it is drunk at one place). Two perceptions are added. **The push** (`brain::push_of`): at nightfall the day joins the body's recent days (`moving.recent_keep_permille`) and is weighed against its usual days of the season (`moving.usual_keep_permille`, a slot a season), in a day of need: the tiles walked beyond what its usual yield a tile would have asked for what it gathered, the walk to its nearest drink there and back beyond the usual, both at what walking costs it, and the hunger and thirst left at nightfall beyond the usual; never under nothing. **The surprise** (`Brains::fire_surprise`): at the fire each present says what it gathered and where it gathered most; a grove away from its home (beyond a grove's side) that gave more than the mean of the present who stayed near home and gathered that day, by `moving.surprise_permille` (a quarter of a day of need), is a surprise lived there: a day marked in its notes of that grove and season (`Lived::surprised`), and in its spouse's at the fire, who takes the day by `moving.spouse_day_percent`. The same day, the same season: what is common cancels. Choices made by the AI session, listed in the pull request: the surprise is measured on food only (water enters by the grade); a stayer who gathered nothing tells nothing of near home and is left out (the first control run counted them: 1,804 surprises, moves undone 64 %); "near home" is a grove within a grove's side of the home; the push signed and summed before its floor.

### D54 — After a skirt the body goes on: the rule of the route kept of three
- **Reason**: the project lead, 22 November 2026, on the world's step audit (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/allegement/audit-pas-du-monde-2026-11-22.md`, the steps that change what a body does): "à part S12, on peut absolument toutes les faire", and, on the question the audit left open (look again later or go on after a skirt, the LRTA* question), for going on. Three rules of the route were coded and measured, one commit each: the path budget 1,500 → 500 tiles (S9), the sight in the open 60 → 40 tiles (S10), and the body going on after a skirt (S11). The lead judges on the long run: a rule that halves the food gathered or doubles the deaths is a different game; and a rule that lightens nothing is not worth a change of the game. On 23 November 2026, on the measures below: "on garde S11 toute seule".
- **Consequences**:
  - **S11 — after a skirt the body goes on, up to `skirts_per_order` legs (6) for one order** (`content/movement.ron`, a placeholder, D30): it goes straight on when the way ahead is open, skirts the obstacle again where it turns, and looks again after each leg, never ending a leg on a tile a leg of that order began or ended on (`Body.went`, never stored); the legs spent, it stops and says it sees no way. `skirts_per_order: 1` is the rule before D54 and the default when the content omits it. On screen: a body before a lake whose far shore is out of sight goes round it instead of giving up at its corner; one before a river with no ford in sight walks a leg or two along the bank and stops where it met the river. The way chosen alone, after a try at the other (a walk to the edge tile of the sight square nearest the target by its way and the crow's flight): the crow's flight pulled the body behind the wall or back to the bank's point nearest the target, so it never went round; following the obstacle does.
  - **S9 and S10 measured and set aside by the lead**: the budget and the sight stay at 1,500 and 60. After lightening 4's pre-test (D51 amended) a refused search expands no tile, so neither weighed on the step, and each changed what a body does a little (a longer way round not taken; shorter legs). Measured (release, probe binaries, `--world vivarium --people 25 --tribes 2 --days 250 --cadence 1 --seed 1`, with trials, each commit with its own content; on a loaded machine, so the CPU read by the ratio of the step to the brain, the same code everywhere): step/brain 1.14 (lightening 4) → 1.16 (S9) → 1.08 (S10) → 1.25 (S11); searches a body-day 15.3 → 15.5 → 16.7 → 17.8; skirts 6.7 → 8.0 → 6.4 → 12.3 % of the searches, stops for no way 6.6 → 8.0 → 6.5 → 4.9 %; alive 67 → 74 → 73 → 71 of 73–80 people, deaths 4 → 3 → 5 → 4 (of illness mostly), births 21 → 27 → 28 → 25, 1,620–1,880 MJ gathered a person throughout.
  - **Measured over 1,000 days**, lightening 4's binary against the three rules together (S11 alone was not measured apart; its share is read in the skirts): CPU of the process 440 → 406 s with trials and 419 → 453 s without (the load's noise: the brain, the same code, 106 → 89 s and 123 → 126 s); the step 152 → 146 s and 153 → 168 s for 9 % and 10 % more searches (the bodies go on instead of stopping: skirts 9.3 → 14.7 % and 5.7 → 10.1 % of the searches, stops for no way 9.3 → 7.2 % and 5.6 → 4.8 %), so a search's step 161 → 142 µs and 130 → 130 µs; alive 62 → 55 and 82 → 85, deaths 29 → 29 and 39 → 39, births 41 → 34 and 71 → 74, 6,170 → 6,350–6,680 and 4,640–5,490 → 4,590–5,630 MJ gathered a person. No rule halves the food or doubles the deaths; none lightens the vivarium measurably: S11 spends in legs what the others saved, and stays for what it does on screen.
  - The pages change (D51's invariant does not apply: this is behaviour); protocol and storage unchanged; no dependency; `plan/` untouched.

### D55 — The well: records written once, accesses, the evening fire
- **Reason**: the project lead, 22 November 2026: "Il n'y a qu'une seule mémoire… un seul dossier mémoire. Chaque individu porte en fait un ID de la mémoire et va chercher cette mémoire collective. Personnage 14 ne partage pas sa mémoire, il dit : moi je connais l'ID mémoire 227. Et du coup, chaque nouveau personnage qui a entendu ça a accès à la mémoire numéro 227." And, of discovery: others copy "parce qu'ils l'ont entendu parler". Lot 4 of the simulation (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-4/proposition-feu-memoire-commune.md`, taken whole: "Franchement, je prends tout"). After lightening 3 (D53) each body carried its own 48 places as copies; nothing was dated, nothing said who had seen what, a tribe's knowledge was the sum of what each had walked, and the brain's one act of telepathy was the imitation (D50). The lead's test holds for every rule here: **a perception or a gesture is fine; a disguised outcome rule is not**; nothing is tuned to a result.
- **Consequences**:
  - **One memory, records written once** (`sim::memory`). Every thing known is a **record** in the world's store (`memory::Store`, in `Brains`, not in `World`): numbered, dated, signed by the first who saw it, **never changed**. Four kinds: a **place** looked at (the S9 square, the day, each kind seen there in one of three states by how many of its plants held food worth a step: bare, some, full, thresholds `fire.place_buckets`, so that one berry more does not make a record more); a **water** found; a **trial** judged (the way, the ways as tried, kept or not, the body's will, how it lived before and during); a **night** (below). The store is **indexed by what a record says**: two looks at the same thing the same day find the same number, never two. A body carries **accesses** (`memory::Access`: the record, who it has it from, the day; twelve bytes), never copies; a record is **freed at its last access** (a count of references; a night freed releases what was told at it); **forgetting is dropping an access**; a body dead releases all of its. The store is read by number only: **nothing scans it**, and a body reads its own 48 places as before.
  - **The credit is a function, not a datum**: a listener's credit in what it heard (1 to 100) is judged once, the night it hears it, from the seed, the listener (a strong will believes others less), the teller (its will: prestige), the record's age that night and the night (`memory::credit`, salt `FIRE_CREDIT`), and never stored: asked again, an access gives the same. Only an exception (a liar, a misreading) would be stored; lot 4 has none, and no liar: the first misunderstandings **emerge** from aged records (the lead's expectation).
  - **The evening fire** (`Brains::vigil`). A body that lies down at its camp for the night joins the circle of its camp (the same home at dusk, within `imitate_tiles` of it); whoever sleeps out, collapses on the way or never reaches the camp before dawn misses the night: physics, not a rule, and the vigil costs no sleep ("s'ils sont fatigués, ils dorment"). At dawn, at each camp where two or more lay down, each present **in the order of their ids** tells **what changed for it** that day, at most `told_per_night` (6): its way judged, the water it found, then the places whose state it found other than the record it held (or saw bearing for the first time, or found bare where a rumour said full), by their value from the camp. **The teller passes numbers, not contents**: each thing told is a record found or written once. Each present then hears the others: it keeps the first `heard_by_age` by its credit in them (a child keeps fewer: 6 at two years, 24 grown, `fire.heard_by_age`, capped by `heard_max`), the younger record next, and gains an access to each. The night is a record of the camp's **hearth** (its last `hearth_nights`, 50, a year): "what the tribe knows" is their union. **Since D56 (23 November 2026) the vigil has three times**: the accounts and the lessons as here, then **the decisions**, each present saying, by decreasing will, where it goes tomorrow (D56).
  - **What a listener does with it.** A **place heard** enters its places as what the teller said, worth less by its credit (`Seen::best_kj × credit / 100`), if it is worth remembering from the camp (the ranking of D53, unchanged) and it holds no later record of that place: **one access a place, the latest by its day, the smaller number at a tie**; its own look later than the record is the latest. Arrived there it looks (D53 does it already) and is **corrected at its first look**: what it sees as the record says, the record is its own; otherwise the access is dropped, and a place told full and found bare is a **rumour dispelled** (`News::Dispelled`), told that same night as a new, dated, bare record, so that **the rumour dies by the gesture that made it**: the present replace the old record by the later one, the absent keep the old (the proposal's 226 → 231). A **water heard** joins the drinking places it knows. A **trial heard**, kept or given up, joins its heard ways (`heard_max` of them): a kept one is what imitation and a trial in distress draw from, and every one is a **lesson** that weighs the listener's next choice of a trial (D50, amended on 23 November 2026, PR 1 b: a weight, never a ban, dated, scaled by the listener's will). **The fire never hands the truth**: a listener receives the teller's record (its kinds, its states, its date), which can be wrong (seen from the edge, three days old).
  - **Placeholders** (`content/needs.ron`, section `fire`, D30): `told_per_night` 6, `heard_max` 24, `heard_by_age` (2 → 6, 8 → 12, 14 → 20, 16 → 24), `place_buckets` (1, 4), `hearth_nights` 50, each explained there. Absent, no fire.
  - **Lightness** (the proposal's §3 and §6 bis): the store is indexed, a body never scans it; a place seen by twenty bodies of a tribe is one record, not twenty copies; the store of a tribe is bounded by its hearth (≤ 24 × 50 nights of told records plus what the places hold). The day does not change; the vigil adds, a night and a camp, n × 24 passages of the memory's bar, less than one look. What the memory alone saves of the brain's time is measured on the pair, never promised.
  - **The control, one flag and two paths** (`--no-fire`, the vivarium; 22 November 2026, the coordinator's reading of the first try). With the fire off, nothing is told, heard or written, and bodies lying down at their camp **imitate as before this decision**: the imitation by proximity of D50 as `main` had it (`Brains::imitate_at_camp`: at night at the camp, the mate who fared best over `trial_days`, its mind read, the way drawn among those that differ, `imitate_percent` scaled by its will, `imitate_loss_percent`), kept exactly so that a pair with and without the fire measures the fire and nothing else: hearsay against proximity, not hearsay against nothing. With the fire lit that path never runs: nobody reads another's mind. The judge without the fire hears nobody and falls back on the body's own days before, as before.
  - **Deterministic** (D24): tellers by their ids, told records sorted by value then place, the credit's jitter by `scatter::roll`, no clock. The pages change (a rule of behaviour): outside D51's invariant; `--no-fire` beside `--no-trials` gives the control. **Nothing stored** (D28 unchanged): the minds live in the vivarium alone (D50); when the brain enters the server, the store and the accesses enter with it (a later snapshot format, said then). No protocol change, no dependency.
  - **Decided alone, told** (the proposal's §8 and the build): the night as a bundle (one record covers a vigil); the teller's cap of 6 and the shape of the table by age; the three states and their thresholds; the record made of the look, written at the fire (the places seen stay the drafts of D53, and only the things told become records); one access a place, the latest by day then the smaller number; the credit as a function; for the relative judge, the whole mean of the heard days, kJ in per cent of need, the bit "changed lately" (a trial under way, a way copied or kept within `trial_days`) said by each, the return to oneself with no heard day; failures told but without effect on the brain in lot 4; the hearth keeps 50 nights; the fire read at dawn for the night that closed (bodies walk home after nightfall, so a circle read at nightfall would have been empty); the fare days are not counted among the `heard_by_age` things; told things in the order way judged, waters, places by value; a rumour dispelled is told worth what the rumour was; the trial record carries the teller's will when judged (so a dead teller's prestige still weighs); `--no-fire` in the same pull request as the rule (its control could not wait); the number D55 (D54 is lightening 4's, in flight).
- **Amended on 23 November 2026 — lot 5a, the hearth tells the truth better** (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/BRIEF-lot-5a-perception.md`; the lead, reading the ten-seed series: "énormément de personnages numéro X est allé voir ce que personnage numéro XY a dit, mais n'a rien trouvé… très très peu efficace"; his yes to the three perception fixes, each built only if its cause was confirmed first, `LOT5A-causes.md`). Measured on seed 2, fire without trials, two years, before any change: a told place was found bare at **22 %** of the looks that met it, **21 % for the first** who came and **42 % from the sixth**, each told place heard by 11.6 listeners; the bare share hardly moved with the record's age (33 % a day old, 40–49 % at four to six days) but went from **6 % (blackberries) to 73–78 % (linden leaves, asparagus)** by what the place bore; **of 6,728 rumours dispelled, 431 were still among the finder's places at the fire and 199 told**, each record dispelled 5.2 times. Two rules change, a perception and a gesture, nothing tuned to a result:
  - **A place heard ages as fast as what it bears.** The credit stays a function (`memory::credit_lasting`), never stored, its jitter the night's it was heard; the record's age is now counted to each dawn, not to the night heard (a record told the evening it was seen was believed whole), and takes its 50 points over the days a told state of the kind lasts, not ten for all: `fire.told_days_per_hour` (4) for each hour of work one of its plants holds in a usual year (its crop's `grams` over `per_hour_g`, `crops.ron`), at most `fire.told_days_max` (20) and the days its crop has left in its season (`crops::days_left`), at least one; a place lasts as its longest-lasting kind told bearing. Hazelnuts (2.5 hours a tree) keep lot 4's ten days; dandelion leaves (8 minutes a plant) are old news by the next dawn. Each dawn after the fire the places heard and not yet seen are believed at that day's credit (`Brains::age_heard`); a dead teller's news keeps the will it had (`gone_wills`), as a trial record carries its teller's. Ways and waters keep the ten days (`memory::TRUST_DAYS`).
  - **A denial takes no slot.** The look that finds a place bare where the record held said food (a rumour dispelled, or the body's own record) keeps a **denial** apart from its 48 places, which forget a bare place first; at the next fire it is told as a bare record **besides** `told_per_night`, at most `fire.denied_per_night` (6), the rumours worth most first (four nights' worth kept while away), and heard **only by the present who hold a record of that place**: the later replaces the older (the 226 → 231 rule), a listener who never held the place gains nothing and spends nothing of its `heard_by_age`. It never displaces a piece of good news; a place seen bearing again since is told among the places instead.
  - **Not built: "tell it as you left it".** The told state is already the place as last read: each choice standing there reads it again (`pick_in_place`) and each look on the way refreshes it; only the last gather before leaving is missed. Of 25,380 places told bearing, **223 (0.9 %)** had been emptied by the teller itself that day: the cause is not confirmed, so the fix was not built (the brief's rule).
  - **Measured** (the lab's `etalon-dix-graines` plan, two tribes of 25, twenty years, seeds 1 to 10, against the series of the same morning; and a control with the ageing alone; `LOT5A-dix-graines.html`): trips to a told place found bare **−44 % with trials and −48 % without, 10 seeds of 10** each, the ageing alone already −51 % and −53 %; with trials and the fire, births 64.2 → 69.4 (8 of 10, none worse), alive at twenty years 76.3 → 79.9 and food carried at noon +13 % (7 of 10 each), kept trials 76 → 96, these gains coming with the denial (the ageing alone: births 4 better and 4 worse, food −3 %); without trials, births 80.9 → 75.8 (7 of 10 worse, from the ageing) and one tribe of seed 6 falls from 48 to 9 in a spring (its known bearing places 2.5 a body against 12 to 14 with the ageing alone; the ageing alone has its own fall, seed 1, 31 deaths of want): the fire without trials stays fragile, as it was (seeds 1, 2 and 4 with 32,000 to 49,000 thin hours before). The twenty no-fire runs are identical to the bit. Time within the shared machine's noise (seed 1 alone, alternated: `main` 334 and 370 s, lot 5a 411 and 365 s). The pages change (behaviour, outside D51's invariant); no protocol, storage, dependency or `plan/` change.
- **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 a) — the circle gathers by place, not by tile.** The project lead: neighbours living a few metres apart must share one fire. A body lying down at its camp joins the circle of its home's **place** (`place_tiles`, the notebook's 16-tile square), no longer of its home's exact tile; the circle's camp, its hearth's key and its night record's `camp` are the place's middle. Two homes on either side of a place's edge still make two fires (decided alone: the notebook's grid, the simplest that fits). **"The fire" is only a name** for the evening gathering at a home: no fire object exists in the simulation; making fire is a later invention.
- **Amended on 23 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 1 ter) — a fifth kind of record: who is believed to know.** `Record::Reputed { who, activity, day }`, "32 knows where berries are", dated by the day the belief was last renewed, deduplicated like the others (one record for every body that renewed the same belief that day). A body holds at most one a reputed activity (`Mind.reputed`, a `Belief`: the access's twelve bytes, the activity's one, its own credit's one, sixteen with padding); the credit is its own, written when renewed and fading by a function of the record's day (`reputation.fade_per_day`), never a shared value. Its witness is nobody: no one wrote it first. Why in the well rather than in the mind: the lead's model is one memory with ids held ("14 connaît la mémoire 227"); a belief renewed the same day by ten bodies is one record. What forms and uses it: D56, amended the same day.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 b) — the move called at the fire.** The caller tells the gain its own notebook gives the new home over its old one ([`fare`], per mille of a day of need), at its turn in the fire's speaking order; the listeners who speak after it weigh it (D56). The gain is told in the call itself: no `Record::Dwelt` is written yet, since nobody would read it after the evening; it comes with the visit that tells it at the old camp (PR 2 c), which needs a record that lasts. Reason: the plan of PR 2 validated by the project lead on 24 November 2026, with only the call in this PR.

### D56 — The decisions at the evening fire: who goes where tomorrow, groups, the look out, the habit
- **Reason**: the project lead, 23 November 2026, on the proposal of lot 5b (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/proposition-veillee-trois-temps.md`): "Je suis d'accord avec toutes tes propositions, on essaye. S'il y a quelque chose qui marche pas, on le modifiera après." After lot 4 (D55) the fire told every listener the same full places, and the next morning the whole tribe ran to the same bush: nobody said where it was going. The fire gets a third time, after the accounts and the lessons: **the decisions**, each said by one present, from its own memory and its own body. The lead's test of D55 holds for every rule here: **a perception or a gesture, never a disguised outcome**; no AI, no levels, nothing tuned to a result. The lead's six answers (the proposal's §13): the speaking order by will; a majority moves the whole camp (PR 2); the common heap counts in "should I gather" (PR 3); putting down at camp is a way, `keep_kj` (PR 3); meetings between tribes too (PR 4); a child follows its mother's decision until 12. This decision covers lot 5b's PR 1; the later pull requests extend it.
- **Consequences**:
  - **Read at dawn, in the same vigil** (`Brains::vigil`, D55): after the accounts and the lessons, each present speaks once, **by decreasing will**, the smaller id at a tie; nobody changes its mind after speaking. Nothing reads the world: each speaks from its 48 places, its accesses and its body.
  - **What one says** (`Brains::fire_decide`, `Act`): it weighs its `decisions.choices` (3) best places bearing food now from the camp, by the worth of D53 with the distance counted **at its own pace** (a child under 12, the old, the weak walk at 70 %: a far place is farther for them), each with the **room** it sees there (what holds there by its memory ÷ `plants_per_gatherer` (2), between 1 and `group_max` (6)). It takes the first of them that is **a call with room left** (it joins: `joined`), or free and **seen by itself** (it calls: `called`), or free and **only heard of** (it goes alone: `alone`). A place taken whole is passed over: **the rush on a told place stops there**. A call nobody joins is a day alone. One who carries its stock and a day's need on top says nothing and keeps its own day (`nothing`); so does the absent, and every body with the decisions off.
  - **The look out** (`looked`): before choosing, one says "I go looking", drawn from the seed (`FIRE_LOOK`) at `look_percent_by_will` (0 → 0 %, 100 → 40 %), twice as often when nobody told a full place that evening, weighed by its habit (100 + `habit_weight` (50) × (look outs that found − look outs that did not), floored at `habit_floor` (10) %). It walks `look_legs` (3) legs out from the camp toward its least known eighth of the compass, each a sight farther than the last, looking from each leg's end, then gathers as any day. A place it finds and tells at the fire to a listener who did not know it counts as **found** (`News::Found`).
  - **A child under `follows_mother_years` (12) whose mother is at the fire** takes her decision: her group, her place alone, or nothing.
  - **The groups** (`Group`, one per caller, for the day). At dawn the caller waits, an hour at most, while a member still sleeps or has not come within `FOLLOW_TILES` (12) of it; a member awake goes to stand by its caller and waits, looking up every `GROUP_WAIT_SECONDS` (4 s). When the caller sets off, a member within reach **walks behind it** (`Intention::Follow`, a gesture of the world: the leader's way copied whole, no search of its own); one out of reach walks to the place by itself. The group goes on until the caller is there, full or not. There each takes **a plant no mate took**; the place bare, the caller goes on to its next best place from there and those who still see it follow; nothing to go on to, the group ends (`bare`). The caller calls the group home when it is full itself or when `home_when_full_percent` (50 %) of those still with it are; a member full before that waits by the caller while it sees it, otherwise leaves. Thirst, hunger with nothing on oneself, the night, the caller's death undo a group as they undo a day. At nightfall each group is told (`News::GroupEnded`): its members, who followed, who was full, who left, how it ended, its stretch.
  - **The habit** (`Mind.habit`): one byte a day, the last `habit_days` (50): what it said (the low four bits, `Act::code`), whether it found a place (`HABIT_FOUND`), whether it found one bare (`HABIT_BARE`), whether it gathered a day of its need (`HABIT_FED`); bit 7 free. It weighs the look out now, and is the one structure the will's temperance and the vocations (PR 1 bis) read.
  - **Placeholders** (`content/needs.ron`, section `decisions`, D30): `choices` 3 (0: no decisions), `plants_per_gatherer` 2, `group_max` 6, `follows_mother_years` 12, `look_percent_by_will` [(0, 0), (100, 40)], `look_legs` 3, `habit_days` 50, `habit_weight` 50, `habit_floor` 10, `home_when_full_percent` 50, each explained there. Absent, no decisions.
  - **The control**: `--no-decisions` (the vivarium) sets the section to its default; the fire tells and teaches as in D55, nobody decides, every day is one's own as before.
  - **Three deviations from the proposal, told before the build and accepted** (the coordinator, 23 November 2026): (1) `Follow` copies the leader's way when the follower stands within reach of it (12 tiles, not a trail of 16 tiles), and **the leader does not wait on the way** for the last: the rule "look behind" waits for the measure of the groups' stretch; (2) **a day's worth for me is weighed by the pace only**: the free load did not change the order of one's own places, so it had no effect and was dropped; (3) **a place only heard of takes room too** (its told state ÷ 2), or the rush would stay whole on heard places; one goes there alone, without a leader.
  - **Decided alone, told** (the build, 23 November 2026): the dawn wait of the caller and of its members (in the first try, members woke after their caller had gone and only 2.6 % of them walked behind it); one carrying its stock and a day's need says nothing (else a caller full at dawn called its group home before it set off); the group goes on until the caller is there; the caller full calls home too; each member looks at the place itself (the proposal's one look for the group was not built: each takes a plant no mate took); the section `decisions` of its own rather than in `fire` and `trials`; the habit's byte; the number D56.
  - **Measured, the five-year try** (band of 25 a tribe, 15 adults, 7 children, 3 infants, two tribes, 250 days, seed 1, cadence 1, one binary, with and without `--no-decisions`, on a machine shared with another series): with / without: alive 61 / 62 at the end, dead 5 / 6 (illness and childbirth, none of want), births 14 / 16, gathered 112.3 / 110.5 million kJ, eaten 101.5 / 101.7, spoiled 5.3 / 5.6, thin person-hours 0 / 0; **the food the tribes carried at noon 1,625 / 1,357 thousand kJ** (the mean of the ten tribe-years, +20 %); places gone to and found bare 9,222 / 9,814 (−6 %); places found and told new 13,169 / 10,133 (+30 %; 1,378 by the look outs, in 564 days of looking); rumours dispelled 14,497 / 13,729. **What was said**, 629 evenings (616 with a full place told): alone 3,717, called 649, joined 1,190, looked 568, nothing 4,421; 3,985 choices passed over because the place was taken whole, 164 calls filled. **The groups**: 648 formed, 1,837 members; 357 of the 1,189 who joined walked behind their caller (30 %; 2.6 % before the dawn wait); 724 full, 515 left on the way; ended home 408, bare 225 (90 went on to a next place), night 15; **the stretch**, caller to last member at the place, over the 132 groups where both arrived: 181 s on average, 1,048 s at most. The run's time is not read from this pair (the machine ran another series; 74.7 / 73.2 s of wall): the ten-seed series measures it.
  - **The mothers**: the proposal promised that mothers of infants stay near the camp by the worth of their day, without a rule. **The pace alone does not do it**: a carried infant does not slow its mother in the model (`pace_percent` reads weakness and age only), so her far places are worth as much as anyone's. Measured at noon, the mean distance to the camp, on each axis the larger: **mothers of infants 152 tiles, other adults 146, with the decisions; 168 and 153 without**; a mother is as far as the others or farther in 9 of the 10 tribe-years with the decisions, in 7 of 10 without. No rule "mothers stay" was added (the coordinator's condition).
  - **Two rules more, decided by the project lead on 23 November 2026** (on the measures above, the coordinator's two recommendations): (1) **carrying an infant slows its carrier**: one carrying an infant walks and gathers at `ages.carrying_pace_percent` (75 %) of its pace (`content/needs.ron`, D30), a gesture of the body in `World::grow`, never a rule "the mothers stay"; its far places weigh less at the fire as for any slow walker (deviation 2); (2) **look behind**: on its way, the caller who **sees** a member not yet there **walking** behind it, farther than `decisions.behind_tiles` (24) and within its sight, stops where it stands and waits, `behind_wait_minutes` (30) a day at most, looking again every `GROUP_WAIT_SECONDS`; it cannot wait for one it does not see, nor for one standing still (drinking, eating). The vivarium counts the members who stood at the place within `TOGETHER_SECONDS` (60) of their caller (`together`) and the callers' waits. **Measured, the five-year try, one seed, each rule alone then both** (PR 1 / carrying alone / looking behind alone / both): alive at the year's end 59 / 60 / 59 / 61; gathered 112.3 / 112.0 / 109.1 / 110.4 million kJ; carried at noon 1,625 / 1,689 / 1,188 / 1,279 thousand kJ; groups 648 / 655 / 473 / 702, ending home 408 / 372 / 206 / 308 and bare 225 / 267 / 265 / 383; those who joined walking behind their caller 30 / 27 / 27 / 25 %; the stretch 181 / 225 / 158 / 174 s on average over 132 / 114 / 62 / 95 groups; **the mothers of infants 152 / 182 / 132 / 198 tiles from the camp at noon against 146 / 149 / 117 / 129 for the other adults**. Read on one seed: the slower pace does **not** bring the mothers nearer: they go as far (their choice of a place by day, D53, does not weigh the pace) and stay out longer; looking behind shortens the stretch but its waits cost the groups their day (fewer come home full, the food carried at noon falls by a quarter). The ten-seed series says whether it holds.
  - **A fact of before, read in the groups**: about 15 % of the walks to a place stop short of it (the way not found: D31, D54), 11 % of the callers' with the decisions on; a group whose caller stops short ends `bare` before it is there.
  - **Measured on ten seeds** (the lab's `etalon-dix-graines` plan: two tribes of 25, twenty years, seeds 1 to 10, with and without trials, against the lot 5a series of the same day, merged as main a706d0b). **Without trials, better**: alive at twenty years 82.6 → 88.5 (6 seeds of 10 higher; seed 6, where a tribe fell, 43 → 86), births 75.8 → 79.7 (8 of 10), deaths of want 6.3 → 2.7, food carried at noon 1,574 → 1,893 thousand kJ (+20 %, 10 of 10), gathered 605 → 630 million kJ. **With trials, slightly worse**: alive 79.9 → 77.5 (8 of 10 lower), births 69.4 → 66.6 (7 of 10), deaths unchanged (39.5 → 39.1), carried at noon 2,663 → 2,824 (4 higher, 6 lower), trials kept 96 → 82.5. The lost births go with sleeping out: the share of minds sleeping out rose from 27 % to 33 %, and over the ten seeds its change and the change of births correlate at −0.70 (the conception asks for the spouse within reach at the season's dawn, D49); supposed here, shown on lot 5b's PR 1 bis control (sleeping out cut: births +10 %), whose conception over the season's first dawns answers it. A run is 17 % faster (1,205 → 1,004 s). A control with the decisions off (`--no-decisions`, seed 1) is not the reference itself: the infant carried slows its carrier there too.
  - **Deterministic** (D24): speakers by will then id, choices by worth then place, the look out by `scatter::roll` (`FIRE_LOOK`), no clock. The pages change (a rule of behaviour, outside D51's invariant). **Nothing stored** (D28 unchanged): the plans, groups and habits live in the vivarium's minds alone (D50). `Intention::Follow` is the simulation's own: **no protocol change** (no client asks it). No dependency; `plan/` untouched.
  - **Amended on 23 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 1 bis) — a confidence by activity; the vocations, downstream, never assigned.** The project lead: "on ne veut jamais définir un métier tel quel… que les PNJ en viennent à se définir eux-mêmes… pas en amont, mais en aval, par conséquence". Each mind holds **a confidence by activity** of the content's list (`temperance.activities`, D30: today `explore`, `gather`, `stay`; later hunting, crafting), each moved by **the will's four rules** (D50, amended the same day: failures borne by innate will, a late success weighing more, the comeback to the innate will each season, being joined at the fire raising its gathering), each with its own streak, each starting at the innate will. **A day is judged from the world, objectively**, at nightfall (`News::Judged`): a look out by whether it found a place and told it new at the next dawn's fire (judged the next nightfall, the fire being read at dawn); a walk to a place (alone, called, joined) by whether it gathered a day of its need; a silence at the fire by whether none of its children under `follows_mother_years` was hungry at night (one without such a child is not judged). Each activity's success rate is a placeholder read on the five-year try of PR 1 bis (explore 75 %, gather 50 %, stay 90 %), so that average luck keeps a confidence where it is and a confidence says one's luck against the others'. **The choice at the fire leans** toward the more confident: the chance to go looking is weighed by its confidence in exploring against its confidence in gathering, ((explore + 5) / (gather + 5), between `habit_floor` % and three times): **a weight, never a ban**; it replaces PR 1's weight of the habit's look outs, which the habit now feeds through the judged days (one structure, the byte of PR 1 unchanged). Staying has no choice to lean yet: it gets one with the common heap (PR 3). **A vocation is only a word in the lab**: the activity of the highest confidence, over the years. The vivarium writes each one's will and confidences at each year's end, its days judged a success or a failure by activity, and the bold of each tribe-year (`docs/10`). **Decided alone, told**: the rarities read on the try rather than guessed (explore was first set at 30 %: its confidence rose for everyone); stay judged by the children only; the look out judged a day late; the lean as a ratio to gathering. Measured, the same twenty years: days judged explore 826 successes / 403 failures (67 %, under its 75 %: the confidence in exploring drifts down for most, and the look outs fall from 1,571 to 1,232), gather 11,656 / 11,631 (50 %), stay 5,621 / 421 (93 %); the vocation at the end, the activity of the highest confidence, gathering for 51, staying for 31, exploring for none. The five-year try (two tribes of 25, 250 days, seed 1, against PR 1): alive 59 / 59 at the year's end, gathered 109.2 / 112.3 million kJ, looked 304 / 568 days, the bold 4 / 1 at the end.
  - **Amended on 23 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 1 bis, after its causes) — the habit leads, the confidence weighs; a success is worth its odds.** The causes, each proved by a control of one change (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/LOT5B-1bis-causes.md`): a day's success gained what keeps the average flat, so almost nothing where successes are common (0.02 point for a look out that found, none for a quiet night); the confidence in exploring moved only by bad luck and the comeback to the innate will, and the lean to look stayed the innate one (tribe A looked 41 % less, tribe B 67 % more, on four seeds of four); the rarity itself changed nothing (75 or 67 %: the same run). The project lead: "je suis d'accord avec tout". (1) **The chance to go looking is again the habit's weight of PR 1** (each look out that found adds `habit_weight`, each one for nothing takes it away), **times** the confidence ratio above (between `habit_floor` % and three times), never under `habit_floor`: it replaces the ratio alone. (2) **A day that succeeds gains one failure's cost times the odds against it**, `failure_milli` × (100 − rarity) / rarity (`Temperance::day_success_milli`: a third of a point for a place found, one for a day fed, a ninth for a quiet night); failures cost as before; the will keeps its own gain (`success_milli`). Average luck now raises a confidence a little (the failures borne free cost nothing); the season's comeback to the innate will holds it.
  - **Measured, PR 1 bis whole and change by change** (the standard plan's runs, two tribes of 25, twenty years, seeds 1 to 4, against #327 on the same seeds; each change added alone on the one before, its own control): alive at twenty years #327 77.8; the will tempered alone 73.2; with the habit leading and the season's first dawns 79.2 (births 68.2 → 78.8, food carried at noon 2,434 → 3,180 thousand kJ, 4 of 4, but one seed's famine: 35 dead of hunger); with a success worth its odds 84.5 (deaths of want 10.0 → 1.8); with the slow ways on their horizon **90.8** (4 of 4 above #327: births 81.5, carried at noon 3,442, gathered 623 → 675 million kJ, days looking 1,620 → 3,048, deaths of want 0.2). Without trials, the same build lives better too (#327 87.0 → 93.5 alive, 4 of 4): the dawns help every wife. **The price of trying** (with trials less without, the same four seeds) falls from −9.3 alive and −9.0 births (#327) to **−2.8 and −4.3** on those four seeds. **On the ten seeds** (the standard plan, with and without trials, against #327's series): alive 77.5 → **88.4** with trials and 88.5 → **96.8** without (10 of 10 both), births 66.6 → 80.0 and 79.7 → 88.2 (10 of 10), gathered 620 → 664 (9 of 10) and 629 → 671 million kJ (10 of 10), days looking 1,537 → 2,878 with trials (10 of 10) but 3,498 → 2,669 without (10 of 10 lower: without trials, a day fed weighs three times a place found); food carried at noon 2,824 → 2,860 with trials (6 of 10: the four seeds' +41 % was their luck) and 1,893 → 1,989 without (10 of 10); deaths 39.1 → 41.6, of illness, with more alive. The price of trying on ten seeds: −11.0 → **−8.4** alive, −13.1 → −8.2 births. A run is 23 % longer (more people). The horizon in days is a season of the vivarium's fifty-day year; at 13 days the four seeds gave 81.5 alive against 90.8, a gap with no mechanism found, read as the four seeds' noise until measured on ten.
  - **Amended on 23 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 1 ter) — who is believed to know, the questions, the evening's review.** The project lead: "on peut mettre ça en place... les améliorer et les pousser un peu plus loin". Three gestures at the fire, each a belief or a dated fact, never a level or a title (D30): (1) **reputation** (`content/needs.ron`, section `reputation`): each body holds, for each of `activities` (explore, gather), the one it believes best there with its own credit, raised by a day it followed one and came back fed (`seen_gain` 20) or an account heard (`heard_gain` 10), worn by a day it came back empty (`wear` 15), fading a point a day; it joins first the call of whom it believes best at gathering (`join_percent`); the belief is a `Record::Reputed` of the common memory (D55); (2) **the questions**: one with no place to go asks whom it believes best "where is food?" and gets that one's best place, `questions_per_night` (4) a fire, the success of the answer counting as the answerer's; (3) **the evening's review** (section `decisions`): a caller keeps `facts_kept` (32) dated facts on its companions ("I waited for 14 on the 12th"), judges each on its last `review_outings` (3) outings, sets aside for tomorrow one with `review_bad` (2) bad ones (a failure of its confidence in gathering) and calls first by name one with two good ones; a parent never sets aside its own child; one set aside weighs first the places within `near_camp_tiles` (40) of the camp. `decisions.review_parts` (set_aside, aside_costs, names) lets a run hold to part of it, all by default. A day's success after successes in a row weighs more (`temperance.streak_percent`, up to twice after four). **Measured on ten seeds against #329** (two tribes of 25, twenty years; `content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/compte-rendu-1ter-causes-2026-11-23.html`): alive 88.4 → 88.7 with trials, 96.8 → 98.3 without; deaths −1.9 with trials (9 of 10); the review whole is the only version without a collapse on ten seeds, its parts alone are not (the review decomposed, same folder). The vivarium's JSON gains `bilan` and `seasons` (docs/10).
  - **The plan for PR 2, amended on 24 November 2026 — no vote, no group rule: each listener decides for itself.** The project lead replaced "a majority moves the whole camp": a trusted person comes to the camp's fire and tells of a better place; each listener (a family, a lone man, a lone woman) decides for itself, by its trust in the teller (reputation), its own lived grade of where it lives (D53, the notebook) and its company. "Sur les 20 personnes qui l'écoutent, il y en a 15 qui sont d'accord et qui y vont. Mais ils n'ont pas choisi comme groupe, c'est plusieurs individualités." **Company becomes a sense of the notebook** (one more `brain::Sense`, D53): how many it lived among at a place enters the place's grade by itself, so a place the others went to draws by what the body lives, not by a count of votes; and some go and check the place before the others follow (testers, a trial judged as any other, D50). Nothing of it is built yet. Today (code reading, 24 November 2026), the fire's circle is keyed on each body's exact home at dusk: those who moved never hear nor tell at the camp's fire, only at their new home's, and only when two or more lie there.
  - **Also for PR 2, with the scouts (24 November 2026): telling the way.** One who found the way past water or rock tells it at the fire, the ford as a place, and a listener walks via it in two legs; until then a mark of seen, not reached (D53) clears only under the body's own feet.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 b) — the call to move, each one's balance, the held goal.** Built as the project lead decided it (no vote; the call is the call/join gesture applied to the home; the caller goes even alone; one home at a time). **The call**: at most one a fire an evening, by the most willing present holding a home its notebook gave (`Act::Moves`; D50). **Each listener present weighs it at its own balance** (the call is the evening's first word: see the couples below): the gain told × its trust in the caller / 100, plus the company it would lack here less the company it would lack there, by its own sociability (D53); here, the present who do not go; there, those who already said they go, the caller first, with their children. Above a hundredth of a day (the margin of `lived_home`) it joins (`Act::Joined`, its gathering decision given up for the day); otherwise it stays. Its trust is its credit in the caller at a new reputation activity, `live`, if it believes the caller best at it (moved by its own home trial's verdict: `seen_gain` kept, `wear` given up; never asked "where is food?"), else the credit of a teller (D55). A child under `follows_mother_years` follows its mother as ever; one under sixteen or under a trial does not weigh it. Who joins walks behind the caller as a group (`Group.moves`), with the wait at dawn and the look behind; the group ends at the new home, where each lives its day. **The couples (the project lead's decision of 24 November 2026)**: the existing couples stay (D49), because modelling looser early-human bonds would be complicated and would touch sensitive subjects; each spouse still decides alone at its own balance, but in its company term the spouse counts as `company.spouse_counts` companions (D53): a spouse who goes pulls the other, which can still stay. **The held goal** (`Mind.goal`, at most one): for now only walking to the new home when it lies farther than a day's walk (the body's pace on the reference ground through the hours of light). By day it comes before the day's decision; drinking and eating interrupt it, and at night the body lies down where it stands; then it goes on. The body says stop: half the load it set off with gone, hungry with nothing to eat on it, thirsty with no water it knows within a day; nothing counts the days. Stopped, the move is given up (D50). `GoalTo` is an enum so that telling at a camp and testing a place (PR 2 c) plug in. Choices made by the AI session, listed in the pull request: no maximum duration; no gathering on the way; the most willing caller first. **The call is the evening's first word (the project lead, 24 November 2026)**: in the plan the call came at its caller's turn in the order of will, and those who had spoken before it never heard it; in this PR's control run (seed 9, five years) 15 of 19 couples split that way, births 30 against 38 on main. The lead: a split for so trivial a cause, a mere order of speaking, goes against keeping couples together, and couples kept together, living at a better home, are what should raise births; so the call is said first and everyone present hears it, the others keeping their order. Reason: the plan of PR 2 validated by the project lead on 24 November 2026 (version "the call and the visit", this PR its part (b)); every rule a perception or a gesture: the caller's notes, the listener's trust and company, the body's own stop.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 b) — the spouse under a trial hears, the absent spouse waits.** The project lead, 24 November 2026: a couple decides together, so **a spouse under a trial hears the call** and weighs it like anyone; if it joins, its trial ends unjudged (no will changed, no credit moved) and it opens the move's home trial; others under a trial still do not weigh it. And "s'il n'est pas avec son conjoint pour faire la décision, il reporte la décision, en attendant la présence de son conjoint": **a listener whose living grown spouse is not at the fire keeps the call** (`Mind.kept_call`: the place, the gain told, the caller, those already going) without weighing it. At the first fire where both are present, before any other word, both weigh it, the holder trusting the first caller, the spouse trusting the holder, each counting the other as `company.spouse_counts` companions (D53); who joins opens its own home trial there, with the held goal if the place lies beyond a day; there is no group with the first caller. The kept call is dropped when the spouse dies or a newer call is heard. A spouse who hears and says no still stays. Choices made by the AI session, listed in the pull request: any living spouse lets a body under a trial hear; those going are counted when the call is kept; in a retold call the more willing of the two weighs first and the second, if it joins, walks behind the first; a dead spouse's kept call is dropped at the next fire. Measured (seed 9, five years): 9 calls, all in tribe B in its fifth year, 2 called and 7 retold; of 20 weighings 3 joined; 8 listeners kept a call for an absent spouse, and the 7 retold calls gave 1 couple moved together, 1 split (one joined, the other said no) and 5 stayed together. The caller's own spouse, absent from its fire, was not covered by the decision and stayed behind both times: left to the project lead.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 b) — the caller waits for its spouse, and weighs its own going after a no.** The project lead, 24 November 2026: the postponement was meant for the caller too. **A body whose living grown spouse is not at its fire does not call** that evening, nor goes alone at no fire: it keeps the call (`Mind.calling`, the same place and gain) and calls at the first evening fire where its spouse is present, where the spouse weighs it like any listener and can still say no. And **after its spouse says no, the caller weighs its own going** with the listeners' balance (`weigh_move`): the gain it sees there, trusting itself (100), the company it would lack here less there, its spouse counting `company.spouse_counts` and staying. Above the margin it goes alone, as before; otherwise it stays with its spouse: its trial is not opened, the call is kept for a later evening with its spouse present, and those who said "I come" that evening stay too, no trial opened (`News::MoveCalled.own`). Choice made by the AI session: while it holds a call, a body draws no other trial (otherwise a new trial would replace the call).
