Measured (seed 9, five years): one caller of tribe B, its spouse saying no each time, stayed and called again 243 evenings, from day 7 to the end, with a gain of 5 per mille: the kept call never ends, and while held no other trial starts. Left to the project lead.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 b) — a move must repay its cost; who stays forgets** (the project lead, 24 November 2026: "il faut vraiment que le déménagement apporte quelque chose": the bodies better their standard of living and move only to a place that is really better, bringing over the medium and long term more food, more children, more of everything). **A move's gain a day must pass the margin plus its cost spread over a horizon** (`brain::move_cost`): the walk from the body's own home to the place, its energy and its time valued as in the reach grade (D53), plus a day of the body's own gathering lost to settling, over `trials.move_repay_seasons` (one season, 12 days; a D30 placeholder). So a near place a little better is taken, a far place barely better is not. It applies wherever a move is decided: the caller's own choice (`lived_best`), each listener's balance (`weigh_move`: the gain told times its trust, less its own cost from its own home), and the caller's weighing after its spouse's no. No discount for a little-known place (an option for later). **The caller who stays forgets** (the project lead): once it decides to stay with its spouse, its call is dropped and its trial ended unjudged; a better place proposed later, its own or another's, is decided afresh, again as a couple. Measured (seed 9, five years): 83 alive, 0 deaths of want, 39 births; 8 calls; one move of tribe B on day 135 (+398 by the caller's notes, 17 joined, 4 couples split by a chosen no), all 20 home trials given up after their season with the new home graded lower than the old (871 to 1050 against 1035 to 1222); two callers of tribe A stayed for their spouse and forgot.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 — the tale makes others go and see, never move.** The synthesis (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/synthese-penseurs-demenager-2026-11-24.md`, points 4 and 5), validated by the project lead. A surprise lived is told at the fire where it is lived: each other grown present, but the teller's spouse (who took the day), with no notes of its own of that grove (a listener who lived there trusts its own notes) and who believes it (the surprise × its credit in the teller, D55, at the threshold or more), goes to see it today by the chance of its will (`moving.see_percent_by_will`, a fifth for the timid, four fifths for the bold; `FIRE_SEE` from the run's seed): at the fire it says it goes alone to the place where the teller gathered (`Act::Alone`, the existing gesture), the teller's record of the place heard if it held none (`News::GoSee`). Its own day there is then compared at the next fire like anyone's: only days lived make a candidate. **The departure** keeps all of PR 2 b (the call at the fire, each one deciding alone, the spouse counting, waiting for an absent spouse, the caller who stays and forgets, the cost repaid over the horizon), with two changes: the call is made only as D50 amended says, and **a listener who lived at the place called weighs it by its own notes** (`brain::own_gain`, trusting itself), not by the gain told. The caller's trial news (`News::Trying`) is now told when it sets off, no longer when it felt the move; the feeling is `News::MoveFelt`. Measured: on the three seeds, 412 to 648 surprises, 520 to 1,114 days gone to see, 3 to 12 calls (2 to 9 felt, 1 to 6 by a huge surprise alone), 25 to 28 % of the grown dawns pushed; 4 to 10 couples split at a call (one joined, the other said no), and couples apart at the dawns of conception rose (seed 9: 166 against 40; seed 2: 448 against 37), the measured cause of the births lost. Left to the project lead: whether a spouse who stays should follow later, and the push threshold.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 — the family weighs apart from company, more with children.** The project lead, 24 November 2026, after the long runs' partings (spouses whose homes parted after one went and the other said no): "un poids de la famille à part de la compagnie", added to it, "double effet sur une personne qui aime la compagnie et 0 sur une personne qui n'aime pas la compagnie, mais la famille a quand même un impact"; "s'il y a des enfants, il y a encore plus de poids". Why: the spouse's weight lived only in the company term, which a loner does not feel at all and which a crowd saturates (more than two to six others add nothing), so in a village a spouse who went pulled nobody. Now, when a body weighs a move called at the fire (`weigh_move`, the listeners and the caller weighing its own going after its spouse's no), each member of its family it would leave behind costs it, wherever that member stands, whatever its sociability and however many others are around: its spouse `family.spouse_permille` (250, a quarter of a day of need a day), each of its children too young to weigh a move (under sixteen) `family.child_permille` (100), on their mother's side (a child of its own body goes with it and weighs nothing either way). A member who goes pulls it there, one who stays holds it here. It pulls, never forces: a gain worth more still wins, and a no is still a no. The company term is unchanged (`company.spouse_counts` stays): a sociable body feels both. D30 placeholders in `content/needs.ron`, section `family`; absent, the family weighs only as company. Storage and protocol unchanged.
